    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the winter demand-response programme reports from a season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from datetime import datetime
    
    LABELS = ("SAFETY_HOLD", "COOLDOWN", "CONTINUITY_WAIVED", 
"CERTIFICATE_PENDING", "CLEAR")
    
    
    def rhu(num, den):
        """num/den rounded half up (num >= 0, den > 0)."""
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def rhe(num, den):
        """num/den rounded half to even (num >= 0, den > 0)."""
        q, r = divmod(num, den)
        two_r = 2 * r
        if two_r > den or (two_r == den and q % 2):
            q += 1
        return q
    
    
    def raw_lines(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            text = fh.read()
        lines = text.split("\n")
        if lines and lines[-1] == "":
            lines.pop()
        return lines
    
    
    def content_lines(path):
        """Non-comment, non-blank lines of a fixed-width or commented text 
file."""
        return [l for l in raw_lines(path) if l.strip() and not 
l.startswith("#")]
    
    
    def csv_rows(path, delimiter=","):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            return list(csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    class Offer:
        __slots__ = ("oid", "event_id", "site_id", "bid", "rel", "rec", "trel", 
"trec",
                     "credit", "segment", "cap_binding")
    
    
    class Extract:
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.counts = {}
            self._load()
    
        def p(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        def _load(self):
            cp = configparser.ConfigParser(interpolation=None)
            with open(self.p("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                cp.read_file(fh)
            units = cp["units"]
            self.wh_per_tick = int(units["wh_per_tick"])
            self.millicents_per_cent = int(units["millicents_per_cent"])
            self.region_of_code = {k.lower(): v for k, v in 
cp["region_codes"].items()}
            self.segment_of_code = {k.lower(): v for k, v in 
cp["segment_codes"].items()}
            self.regions = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segments = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            adders = {k.lower(): int(v) for k, v in 
cp["segment_rate_adders"].items()} \
                if cp.has_section("segment_rate_adders") else {}
            self.segment_adder = {s: adders.get(s.lower(), 0) for s in 
self.segments}
            grants = cp["grants"]
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = int(grants["claim_cents_per_weight"])
            self.minimum_grant_cents = int(grants["minimum_grant_cents"])
    
            # events
            with open(self.p("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                events = json.load(fh)
            self.counts["events.json"] = len(events)
            self.events = {}
            for e in events:
                self.events[e["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": e["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(e["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(e["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": int(e["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "uplift": int(e["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                    "start": datetime.fromisoformat(e["dispatch_start"]),
                }
            self.event_ids = sorted(self.events)
            # season order: by start instant, same instant -> descending 
event_id
            season = sorted(self.events.values(), key=lambda e: e["event_id"], 
reverse=True)
            season.sort(key=lambda e: e["start"])
            self.season_order = [e["event_id"] for e in season]
    
            # sites
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("sites.csv"))
            self.counts["sites.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.site_rows = {}
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                code = (r["region_code"] or "").lower()
                region = self.region_of_code.get(code)
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
                self.site_rows[key] = {
                    "event_id": r["event_id"],
                    "site_id": r["site_id"],
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": self.segment_of_code.get((r["segment_code"] or 
"").lower()),
                    "beneficiary_id": r["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": r["critical"] == "Y",
                }
    
            # site state
            lines = [l for l in raw_lines(self.p("site_state.jsonl")) if 
l.strip()]
            self.counts["site_state.jsonl"] = len(lines)
            for l in lines:
                o = json.loads(l)
                sr = self.site_rows.get((o["event_id"], o["site_id"]))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                sr["dispatch_authorized"] = bool(o["dispatch_authorized"])
                sr["safety_hold"] = bool(o["safety_hold"])
                sr["cooldown"] = bool(o["cooldown"])
    
            # continuity waivers
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("continuity_waivers.csv"))
            self.counts["continuity_waivers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            for r in rows:
                if not (r.get("waiver_signed") or ""):
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                sr = self.site_rows.get((r["event_id"], r["site_id"]))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                sr["cert_pending"] = r["routine_certificate_pending"] == "Y"
                sr["waiver_status"] = r["waiver_status"]
                sr["waiver_signed"] = (r.get("waiver_signed") or "") == "Y"
    
            # rate cards
            root = ET.parse(self.p("rate_cards.xml")).getroot()
            cards = root.findall("card")
            self.counts["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
            for c in cards:
                sr = self.site_rows.get((c.get("event"), c.get("site")))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                override = c.find("override")
                start = int((override if override is not None else 
c.find("base")).text)
                sr["rate_raw_base"] = start
                sr["cap"] = int(c.find("cap").text)
    
            # recipient weights
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.counts["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(rows)
            for r in rows:
                sr = self.site_rows.get((r["event_id"], r["site_id"]))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                sr["grant_weight"] = int(r["grant_weight"])
    
            self._finish_site_rows()
            self._load_offers()
            self._load_profiles()
            self._load_targets()
            self._load_feeder_limits()
            self._load_floors()
            self._load_pools()
            self._load_allowances()
    
            rows = [l for l in content_lines(self.p("export_manifest.tsv"))]
            self.counts["export_manifest.tsv"] = max(0, len(rows) - 1)
    
        def _finish_site_rows(self):
            """Compliance label and effective rate of every site row."""
            for sr in self.site_rows.values():
                ev = self.events[sr["event_id"]]
                waiver_valid = (sr.get("waiver_status") == "VALID" and 
sr.get("waiver_signed")
                                and sr["critical"] and sr["segment"] == 
"public")
                if sr.get("safety_hold"):
                    label = "SAFETY_HOLD"
                elif sr.get("cooldown"):
                    label = "COOLDOWN"
                elif sr.get("cert_pending") and waiver_valid:
                    label = "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
                elif sr.get("cert_pending"):
                    label = "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
                else:
                    label = "CLEAR"
                sr["label"] = label
                raw = sr["rate_raw_base"] + ev["uplift"] + 
self.segment_adder.get(sr["segment"], 0)
                cap = sr["cap"]
                sr["rate_raw"] = raw
                sr["rate"] = cap if raw > cap else raw
                sr["cap_binding"] = raw > cap
    
        def _load_offers(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("offers.csv"))
            self.counts["offers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.offer_event = {}
            self.offer_rows = []
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                self.offer_event[r["offer_id"]] = r["event_id"]
                if (r["event_id"], r["site_id"]) not in self.site_rows:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.offer_rows.append(r)
    
        def _load_profiles(self):
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            lines = raw_lines(self.p("profiles.tsv"))
            header = lines[0].split("\t")
            idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(header)}
            i_off, i_itv = idx["offer_id"], idx["interval"]
            i_rel, i_rec, i_mul = idx["relief_wh"], idx["recovery_wh"], 
idx["register_multiplier"]
            self.counts["profiles.tsv"] = len(lines) - 1
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            prof = {}
            for line in lines[1:]:
                f = line.split("\t")
                oid = f[i_off]
                event_id = self.offer_event.get(oid)
                if event_id is None:
                    self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                itv = int(f[i_itv])
                if itv < 1 or itv > self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
                    self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                slot = prof.setdefault(oid, {})
                if itv in slot:
                    self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                mul = int(f[i_mul])
                slot[itv] = (rhu(int(f[i_rel]) * mul, wpt), rhu(int(f[i_rec]) * 
mul, wpt))
            self.profiles = prof
    
        def _load_targets(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.counts["targets.tsv"] = len(rows)
            self.targets = {}
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_of_code[r["region_code"].lower()]
                self.targets[(r["event_id"], region, int(r["interval"]))] = (
                    rhu(int(r["target_wh"]), self.wh_per_tick), 
int(r["shortfall_weight"]))
    
        def _load_feeder_limits(self):
            lines = content_lines(self.p("feeder_limits.txt"))
            self.counts["feeder_limits.txt"] = len(lines)
            self.ceilings = {}
            for l in lines:
                event_id = l[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_of_code[l[10:14].strip().lower()]
                itv = int(l[14:17])
                self.ceilings[(event_id, region, itv)] = (
                    rhu(int(l[17:27]), self.wh_per_tick), rhu(int(l[27:37]), 
self.wh_per_tick))
    
        def _load_floors(self):
            with open(self.p("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                floors = json.load(fh)["floors"]
            self.counts["segment_floors.json"] = len(floors)
            self.floors = {}
            for f in floors:
                region = self.region_of_code[f["region_code"].lower()]
                segment = self.segment_of_code[f["segment_code"].lower()]
                self.floors[(f["event_id"], region, segment)] = (
                    rhu(int(f["floor_wh"]), self.wh_per_tick), 
int(f["priority_weight"]))
    
        def _load_pools(self):
            lines = content_lines(self.p("pools.txt"))
            self.counts["pools.txt"] = len(lines)
            self.pools = {}
            for l in lines:
                event_id = l[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_of_code[l[10:14].strip().lower()]
                self.pools[(event_id, region)] = int(l[14:26])
    
        def _load_allowances(self):
            lines = content_lines(self.p("site_allowances.tsv"))
            self.counts["site_allowances.tsv"] = max(0, len(lines) - 1)
            header = lines[0].split("\t")
            idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(header)}
            allowance = {}
            draws = {}
            for l in lines[1:]:
                f = l.split("\t")
                site_id = f[idx["site_id"]]
                allowance[site_id] = int(f[idx["allowance_ticks"]])
                src = f[idx["draws_from"]].strip() if idx["draws_from"] < len(f)
else ""
                draws[site_id] = src
            root = {}
    
            def resolve(site_id):
                if site_id in root:
                    return root[site_id]
                chain = []
                cur = site_id
                while draws.get(cur):
                    chain.append(cur)
                    cur = draws[cur]
                for s in chain:
                    root[s] = cur
                root[site_id] = cur
                return cur
    
            self.allowance_root = {s: resolve(s) for s in allowance}
            self.allowance_ticks = allowance
    
    
    def build_offers(ex):
        """Offer objects for every non-orphan offer, with ticks, credit and 
totals."""
        out = []
        den = 1000 * ex.millicents_per_cent
        wpt = ex.wh_per_tick
        for r in ex.offer_rows:
            sr = ex.site_rows[(r["event_id"], r["site_id"])]
            n = ex.events[r["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
            prof = ex.profiles.get(r["offer_id"], {})
            o = Offer()
            o.oid = r["offer_id"]
            o.event_id = r["event_id"]
            o.site_id = r["site_id"]
            o.bid = int(r["bid_cost_cents"])
            o.segment = sr["segment"]
            o.cap_binding = sr["cap_binding"]
            rel = []
            rec = []
            credit = 0
            rate = sr["rate"]
            for t in range(1, n + 1):
                a, b = prof.get(t, (0, 0))
                rel.append(a)
                rec.append(b)
                credited = a - b if a > b else 0
                if credited:
                    credit += rhe(credited * wpt * rate, den)
            o.rel = rel
            o.rec = rec
            o.trel = sum(rel)
            o.trec = sum(rec)
            o.credit = credit
            out.append(o)
        return out
    
    
    def solve(ex, event_id, region, site_options):
        """Pick the best feasible portfolio; return (offers, tie_count)."""
        ev = ex.events[event_id]
        n = ev["interval_count"]
        target = []
        weight = []
        rel_ceil = []
        rec_ceil = []
        for t in range(1, n + 1):
            tg, w = ex.targets[(event_id, region, t)]
            target.append(tg)
            weight.append(w)
            rc, qc = ex.ceilings[(event_id, region, t)]
            rel_ceil.append(rc)
            rec_ceil.append(qc)
        floors = []
        for seg in ex.segments:
            f, pw = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg), (0, 0))
            if f > 0 and pw > 0:
                floors.append((seg, f, pw))
        cost_weight = ev["cost_weight"]
        recovery_weight = ev["recovery_weight"]
        seg_index = {seg: i for i, (seg, _f, _w) in enumerate(floors)}
        nseg = len(floors)
    
        options = []
        for site_id in sorted(site_options):
            options.append([None] + sorted(site_options[site_id], key=lambda o: 
o.oid))
        depth = len(options)
    
        rel = [0] * n
        rec = [0] * n
        segrel = [0] * nseg
        chosen = []
        state = {"bid": 0, "trec": 0}
        best = [None, None, 0]  # key, ids, tie count
    
        def evaluate():
            shortfall = 0
            over = 0
            for t in range(n):
                d = target[t] - rel[t]
                if d > 0:
                    shortfall += weight[t] * d
                elif d < 0:
                    over -= d
            deficit = 0
            for i in range(nseg):
                d = floors[i][1] - segrel[i]
                if d > 0:
                    deficit += floors[i][2] * d
            key = (shortfall, deficit,
                   cost_weight * state["bid"] + recovery_weight * state["trec"],
                   over, len(chosen))
            if best[0] is None or key < best[0]:
                best[0] = key
                best[1] = tuple(sorted(o.oid for o in chosen))
                best[2] = 1
            elif key == best[0]:
                best[2] += 1
                ids = tuple(sorted(o.oid for o in chosen))
                if ids > best[1]:
                    best[1] = ids
    
        def dfs(i):
            if i == depth:
                evaluate()
                return
            for o in options[i]:
                if o is None:
                    dfs(i + 1)
                    continue
                orel = o.rel
                orec = o.rec
                feasible = True
                for t in range(n):
                    if orel[t] and rel[t] + orel[t] > rel_ceil[t]:
                        feasible = False
                        break
                    if orec[t] and rec[t] + orec[t] > rec_ceil[t]:
                        feasible = False
                        break
                if not feasible:
                    continue
                for t in range(n):
                    rel[t] += orel[t]
                    rec[t] += orec[t]
                si = seg_index.get(o.segment)
                if si is not None:
                    segrel[si] += o.trel
                state["bid"] += o.bid
                state["trec"] += o.trec
                chosen.append(o)
                dfs(i + 1)
                chosen.pop()
                state["bid"] -= o.bid
                state["trec"] -= o.trec
                if si is not None:
                    segrel[si] -= o.trel
                for t in range(n):
                    rel[t] -= orel[t]
                    rec[t] -= orec[t]
    
        dfs(0)
        by_id = {o.oid: o for opts in options for o in opts if o is not None}
        return [by_id[i] for i in best[1]], best[2]
    
    
    def largest_remainder(F, weights, order_desc):
        """floor shares plus one-cent remainders; returns (shares, residual, 
tie)."""
        W = sum(weights.values())
        shares = {}
        for b, w in weights.items():
            shares[b] = F * w // W
        left = F - sum(shares.values())
        rem = {b: (F * w) % W for b, w in weights.items()}
        seen = {}
        tie = False
        for b, r in rem.items():
            if r:
                if r in seen:
                    tie = True
                seen[r] = b
        order = list(order_desc)
        order.sort(key=lambda b: -rem[b])
        for k in range(left):
            shares[order[k % len(order)]] += 1
        return shares, left, tie
    
    
    def compute_grants(ex, event_id, region, pool, carried, credit_cents, 
site_rows):
        """Apportion the funded pool; returns a dict of grant figures."""
        F = pool + carried - credit_cents
        if F < 0:
            F = 0
        weights = {}
        recipients = 0
        for sr in site_rows:
            if "grant_weight" not in sr:
                continue
            recipients += 1
            w = sr["grant_weight"]
            if sr["label"] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                w *= 2
            b = sr["beneficiary_id"]
            weights[b] = weights.get(b, 0) + w
        W = sum(weights.values())
        out = {
            "pool": pool, "F": F, "allocated": 0, "unallocated": F, "grants": 
[],
            "residual": 0, "residual_tie": False,
            "zero_denominator": recipients == 0 or W == 0,
        }
        if recipients == 0 or W == 0:
            return out
        positive = {b: w for b, w in weights.items() if w > 0}
        order_desc = sorted(positive, reverse=True)
        claims = {b: w * ex.claim_cents_per_weight for b, w in positive.items()}
        if sum(claims.values()) <= F:
            shares = claims
            residual, tie = 0, False
        else:
            shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(F, positive, order_desc)
            kept = {b: w for b, w in positive.items() if shares[b] >= 
ex.minimum_grant_cents}
            if len(kept) != len(positive):
                if not kept:
                    shares = {b: 0 for b in positive}
                    residual, tie = 0, False
                else:
                    shares2, residual, tie = largest_remainder(
                        F, kept, [b for b in order_desc if b in kept])
                    shares = {b: shares2.get(b, 0) for b in positive}
        allocated = sum(shares.values())
        out["allocated"] = allocated
        out["unallocated"] = F - allocated
        out["grants"] = [{"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares[b]} for b in 
sorted(positive)]
        out["residual"] = residual
        out["residual_tie"] = tie
        return out
    
    
    def run(ex):
        offers = build_offers(ex)
        by_event_region = {}
        for o in offers:
            sr = ex.site_rows[(o.event_id, o.site_id)]
            by_event_region.setdefault((o.event_id, sr["region"]), []).append(o)
        rows_by_event_region = {}
        for sr in ex.site_rows.values():
            rows_by_event_region.setdefault((sr["event_id"], sr["region"]), 
[]).append(sr)
    
        remaining = {s: ex.allowance_ticks[s] for s in 
set(ex.allowance_root.values())}
        carried = {r: 0 for r in ex.regions}
        stats = {"ineligible": 0, "blocked": 0, "empty_groups": 0, 
"cap_binding": 0,
                 "residual": 0, "residual_tie_pools": 0, "unallocated_pools": 0}
        results = {}
    
        for event_id in ex.season_order:
            consumed = []
            for region in ex.regions:
                key = (event_id, region)
                site_options = {}
                for o in by_event_region.get(key, []):
                    sr = ex.site_rows[(o.event_id, o.site_id)]
                    if not sr.get("dispatch_authorized"):
                        stats["ineligible"] += 1
                        continue
                    root = ex.allowance_root.get(o.site_id)
                    left = remaining.get(root, 0)
                    if o.trel > left:
                        stats["blocked"] += 1
                        continue
                    site_options.setdefault(o.site_id, []).append(o)
                candidate_count = sum(len(v) for v in site_options.values())
                if candidate_count == 0:
                    stats["empty_groups"] += 1
                chosen, tie_count = solve(ex, event_id, region, site_options)
                consumed.extend(chosen)
    
                n = ex.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
                rel = [0] * n
                rec = [0] * n
                bid = 0
                credit = 0
                for o in chosen:
                    for t in range(n):
                        rel[t] += o.rel[t]
                        rec[t] += o.rec[t]
                    bid += o.bid
                    credit += o.credit
                    if o.cap_binding:
                        stats["cap_binding"] += 1
    
                site_rows = rows_by_event_region.get(key, [])
                g = compute_grants(ex, event_id, region, ex.pools.get(key, 0),
                                   carried[region], credit, site_rows)
                carried[region] = g["unallocated"]
                stats["residual"] += g["residual"]
                if g["residual_tie"]:
                    stats["residual_tie_pools"] += 1
                if g["zero_denominator"]:
                    stats["unallocated_pools"] += 1
    
                counts = {lab: 0 for lab in LABELS}
                for sr in site_rows:
                    counts[sr["label"]] += 1
    
                results[key] = {
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
                    "chosen": chosen,
                    "rel": rel,
                    "rec": rec,
                    "bid": bid,
                    "credit": credit,
                    "grants": g,
                    "compliance_counts": counts,
                    "tie_count": tie_count,
                }
            for o in consumed:
                root = ex.allowance_root.get(o.site_id)
                if root in remaining:
                    remaining[root] -= o.trel
        return results, stats
    
    
    def region_doc(ex, region, results):
        events_out = []
        totals = {k: 0 for k in ("event_count", "candidate_offer_count", 
"selected_offer_count",
                                 "relief_ticks", "recovery_ticks", 
"shortfall_ticks",
                                 "overdelivery_ticks", "bid_cost_cents", 
"credit_cents",
                                 "grant_pool_cents", "grant_funded_cents",
                                 "grant_allocated_cents", 
"grant_unallocated_cents")}
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            r = results[(event_id, region)]
            n = ex.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
            intervals = []
            rel_sum = rec_sum = short_sum = over_sum = 0
            for t in range(1, n + 1):
                tg = ex.targets[(event_id, region, t)][0]
                rl = r["rel"][t - 1]
                rc = r["rec"][t - 1]
                short = tg - rl if tg > rl else 0
                over = rl - tg if rl > tg else 0
                intervals.append({"interval": t, "target_ticks": tg, 
"relief_ticks": rl,
                                  "recovery_ticks": rc, "shortfall_ticks": 
short,
                                  "overdelivery_ticks": over})
                rel_sum += rl
                rec_sum += rc
                short_sum += short
                over_sum += over
            g = r["grants"]
            entry = {
                "event_id": event_id,
                "candidate_offer_count": r["candidate_offer_count"],
                "selected_offer_ids": sorted(o.oid for o in r["chosen"]),
                "intervals": intervals,
                "bid_cost_cents": r["bid"],
                "credit_cents": r["credit"],
                "grant_pool_cents": g["pool"],
                "grant_funded_cents": g["F"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": g["allocated"],
                "grant_unallocated_cents": g["unallocated"],
                "grants": g["grants"],
                "compliance_counts": r["compliance_counts"],
            }
            events_out.append(entry)
            totals["event_count"] += 1
            totals["candidate_offer_count"] += r["candidate_offer_count"]
            totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(r["chosen"])
            totals["relief_ticks"] += rel_sum
            totals["recovery_ticks"] += rec_sum
            totals["shortfall_ticks"] += short_sum
            totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += over_sum
            totals["bid_cost_cents"] += r["bid"]
            totals["credit_cents"] += r["credit"]
            totals["grant_pool_cents"] += g["pool"]
            totals["grant_funded_cents"] += g["F"]
            totals["grant_allocated_cents"] += g["allocated"]
            totals["grant_unallocated_cents"] += g["unallocated"]
        return {"region": region, "events": events_out, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def segment_doc(ex, segment, results):
        entries = []
        totals = {k: 0 for k in ("selected_offer_count", "relief_ticks", 
"recovery_ticks",
                                 "bid_cost_cents", "credit_cents", 
"floor_ticks")}
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                r = results[(event_id, region)]
                chosen = [o for o in r["chosen"] if o.segment == segment]
                rel = sum(o.trel for o in chosen)
                rec = sum(o.trec for o in chosen)
                bid = sum(o.bid for o in chosen)
                credit = sum(o.credit for o in chosen)
                floor = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0]
                if floor == 0:
                    bp = 10000
                else:
                    bp = min(10000, rhu(10000 * rel, floor))
                entries.append({
                    "event_id": event_id, "region": region,
                    "selected_offer_ids": sorted(o.oid for o in chosen),
                    "relief_ticks": rel, "recovery_ticks": rec,
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid, "credit_cents": credit,
                    "floor_ticks": floor, "floor_attainment_bp": bp,
                })
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(chosen)
                totals["relief_ticks"] += rel
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += rec
                totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid
                totals["credit_cents"] += credit
                totals["floor_ticks"] += floor
        return {"segment": segment, "event_regions": entries, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def summary_doc(ex, results, region_docs, segment_docs):
        keys = ("candidate_offer_count", "selected_offer_count", "relief_ticks",
                "recovery_ticks", "shortfall_ticks", "overdelivery_ticks",
                "bid_cost_cents", "credit_cents", "grant_pool_cents",
                "grant_funded_cents", "grant_allocated_cents", 
"grant_unallocated_cents")
        doc = {k: 0 for k in keys}
        for region in ex.regions:
            t = region_docs[region]["totals"]
            for k in keys:
                doc[k] += t[k]
        doc["event_count"] = len(ex.event_ids)
        doc["event_region_count"] = len(ex.event_ids) * len(ex.regions)
        doc["site_count"] = len(ex.site_rows)
        doc["regions"] = {}
        for region in ex.regions:
            t = region_docs[region]["totals"]
            doc["regions"][region] = {
                "selected_offer_count": t["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": t["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": t["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": t["credit_cents"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": t["grant_allocated_cents"],
            }
        doc["segments"] = {}
        for segment in ex.segments:
            t = segment_docs[segment]["totals"]
            doc["segments"][segment] = {
                "selected_offer_count": t["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": t["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": t["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": t["credit_cents"],
            }
        return doc
    
    
    def policy_doc(ex, results, stats):
        counts = {lab: 0 for lab in LABELS}
        labels = []
        for key in sorted(ex.site_rows):
            sr = ex.site_rows[key]
            counts[sr["label"]] += 1
            labels.append({"event_id": sr["event_id"], "site_id": sr["site_id"],
                           "label": sr["label"]})
        ties = []
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                r = results[(event_id, region)]
                if r["tie_count"] >= 2:
                    ties.append({"event_id": event_id, "region": region,
                                 "tied_portfolio_count": r["tie_count"],
                                 "selected_offer_ids": sorted(o.oid for o in 
r["chosen"])})
        return {
            "compliance_counts": counts,
            "site_labels": labels,
            "tie_decisions": ties,
            "cap_binding_count": stats["cap_binding"],
            "residual_cents": stats["residual"],
            "residual_tie_pool_count": stats["residual_tie_pools"],
        }
    
    
    def quality_doc(ex, stats):
        zero_floor = 0
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                for segment in ex.segments:
                    if ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0] == 
0:
                        zero_floor += 1
        return {
            "rows": dict(ex.counts),
            "orphan_offer_rows": ex.orphan_offer_rows,
            "orphan_profile_rows": ex.orphan_profile_rows,
            "out_of_range_profile_rows": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,
            "duplicate_profile_rows": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,
            "unknown_region_site_rows": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,
            "waiver_signed_missing": ex.waiver_signed_missing,
            "ineligible_offer_count": stats["ineligible"],
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": stats["blocked"],
            "empty_candidate_groups": stats["empty_groups"],
            "zero_floor_count": zero_floor,
            "unallocated_pool_count": stats["unallocated_pools"],
        }
    
    
    def write_json(path, doc):
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(doc, fh, sort_keys=True, indent=1)
            fh.write("\n")
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            print("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        data_dir, out_dir = argv[1], argv[2]
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        ex = Extract(data_dir)
        results, stats = run(ex)
        region_docs = {r: region_doc(ex, r, results) for r in ex.regions}
        segment_docs = {s: segment_doc(ex, s, results) for s in ex.segments}
        for region, doc in region_docs.items():
            write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "region_%s.json" % region), doc)
        for segment, doc in segment_docs.items():
            write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "segment_%s.json" % segment), doc)
        write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"),
                   summary_doc(ex, results, region_docs, segment_docs))
        write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "policy_audit.json"), policy_doc(ex, 
results, stats))
        write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "data_quality.json"), quality_doc(ex, 
stats))
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

Summary: Create derive_report.py implementing the rulebook

Reasoning:
Before recreating, I want to double check my solve function's floor filtering 
logic looks correct, but I'm worried about the leftover distribution in 
largest_remainder — using modulo indexing into the order array breaks if the 
leftover count exceeds the number of beneficiaries.

Since each remainder is strictly less than W and I'm summing across n 
beneficiaries, the leftover total is mathematically bounded below n, so a simple
`for k in range(left): shares[order[k]] += 1` is safe without needing modulo. I 
also want to verify the residual tie-detection logic correctly flags duplicate 
non-zero remainders, and confirm that `zero_denominator` properly maps to 
unallocated pool count in compute_grants — then I still need to check how 
minimum_grant_cents is applied in the claims branch.

I'm checking that event-regions without site rows default to zero counts 
correctly, and that the remaining-allowance mapping handles sites missing from 
site_allowances.tsv by blocking offers with positive trel rather than erroring, 
which the schema guarantees won't happen anyway. I also want to confirm the 
iteration order over offers and tie-breaking logic in solve picks the greatest 
tuple of sorted ids consistently.

I'm then verifying evaluate runs correctly on leaf nodes including the empty 
portfolio, that the pruning loop handles zero-ceiling and zero-relief cases 
without false skips, and that region summaries, shortfall totals, and the JSON 
writer with sorted keys all produce consistent output.

I'm also checking that missing waiver_signed columns default safely to None, 
that site row finishing can safely assume exactly one card per row per schema, 
that event lookups and season ordering handle mixed timezone-aware dates fine, 
and that numeric fields like target_wh stay non-negative throughout the 
credited-weight calculations.

Verifying the credit formula: ticks times wh_per_tick gives Wh, divided by 1000 
gives kWh, times rate gives millicents, divided by millicents_per_cent gives 
cents, so the numerator is credited*wpt*rate and the denominator is 1000 times 
millicents_per_cent. I'm also confirming cap_binding_count only counts chosen 
offers, and since only one offer per site can be selected, there's no 
double-counting risk there.

Checking tie_decisions ordering by event_id then region name, and segment 
event_regions ordering the same way — both line up correctly. For section 5's 
second component, the formula sums priority_weight times the excess of floor 
ticks over segment relief across segments, matching my implementation, and 
segment relief itself is the sum of per-interval ticks for offers at that 
segment's sites, which matches section 8.2's definition too.

I'm verifying consistency between region-level and segment-level relief totals —
since every chosen offer's segment code is always valid, the global total should
equal both the sum across region files and the sum across segment files. One 
edge case is a site with an unknown segment code, which would mean that offer 
isn't counted in any segment file, but the schema doesn't address this.

I confirm the content_lines count for the export manifest (15 total minus 1 
header = 14), and note that the pools field parsing assumes the value is never 
blank. Now I'm ready to fix the largest-remainder leftover loop and write the 
final file.

    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the winter demand-response programme reports from a season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from datetime import datetime
    
    LABELS = ("SAFETY_HOLD", "COOLDOWN", "CONTINUITY_WAIVED", 
"CERTIFICATE_PENDING", "CLEAR")
    
    
    def rhu(num, den):
        """num/den rounded half up (num >= 0, den > 0)."""
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def rhe(num, den):
        """num/den rounded half to even (num >= 0, den > 0)."""
        q, r = divmod(num, den)
        two_r = 2 * r
        if two_r > den or (two_r == den and q % 2):
            q += 1
        return q
    
    
    def raw_lines(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            lines = fh.read().split("\n")
        if lines and lines[-1] == "":
            lines.pop()
        return lines
    
    
    def content_lines(path):
        """Non-comment, non-blank lines of a commented text file."""
        return [l for l in raw_lines(path) if l.strip() and not 
l.startswith("#")]
    
    
    def csv_rows(path, delimiter=","):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            return list(csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    class Offer:
        __slots__ = ("oid", "event_id", "site_id", "bid", "rel", "rec", "trel", 
"trec",
                     "credit", "segment", "cap_binding")
    
    
    class Extract:
        """Everything one season extract says, parsed and quantised."""
    
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.counts = {}
            self._load()
    
        def p(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        def _load(self):
            self._load_program()
            self._load_events()
            self._load_sites()
            self._load_site_state()
            self._load_waivers()
            self._load_rate_cards()
            self._load_recipient_weights()
            self._finish_site_rows()
            self._load_offers()
            self._load_profiles()
            self._load_targets()
            self._load_feeder_limits()
            self._load_floors()
            self._load_pools()
            self._load_allowances()
            self.counts["export_manifest.tsv"] = max(
                0, len(content_lines(self.p("export_manifest.tsv"))) - 1)
    
        def _load_program(self):
            cp = configparser.ConfigParser(interpolation=None)
            with open(self.p("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                cp.read_file(fh)
            units = cp["units"]
            self.wh_per_tick = int(units["wh_per_tick"])
            self.millicents_per_cent = int(units["millicents_per_cent"])
            self.region_of_code = {k.lower(): v for k, v in 
cp["region_codes"].items()}
            self.segment_of_code = {k.lower(): v for k, v in 
cp["segment_codes"].items()}
            self.regions = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segments = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            adders = ({k.lower(): int(v) for k, v in 
cp["segment_rate_adders"].items()}
                      if cp.has_section("segment_rate_adders") else {})
            self.segment_adder = {s: adders.get(s.lower(), 0) for s in 
self.segments}
            grants = cp["grants"]
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = int(grants["claim_cents_per_weight"])
            self.minimum_grant_cents = int(grants["minimum_grant_cents"])
    
        def _load_events(self):
            with open(self.p("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                events = json.load(fh)
            self.counts["events.json"] = len(events)
            self.events = {}
            for e in events:
                self.events[e["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": e["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(e["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(e["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": int(e["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "uplift": int(e["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                    "start": datetime.fromisoformat(e["dispatch_start"]),
                }
            self.event_ids = sorted(self.events)
            season = sorted(self.events.values(), key=lambda e: e["event_id"], 
reverse=True)
            season.sort(key=lambda e: e["start"])
            self.season_order = [e["event_id"] for e in season]
    
        def _load_sites(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("sites.csv"))
            self.counts["sites.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.site_rows = {}
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_of_code.get((r["region_code"] or 
"").lower())
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.site_rows[(r["event_id"], r["site_id"])] = {
                    "event_id": r["event_id"],
                    "site_id": r["site_id"],
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": self.segment_of_code.get((r["segment_code"] or 
"").lower()),
                    "beneficiary_id": r["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": r["critical"] == "Y",
                }
    
        def _load_site_state(self):
            lines = [l for l in raw_lines(self.p("site_state.jsonl")) if 
l.strip()]
            self.counts["site_state.jsonl"] = len(lines)
            for l in lines:
                o = json.loads(l)
                sr = self.site_rows.get((o["event_id"], o["site_id"]))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                sr["dispatch_authorized"] = bool(o["dispatch_authorized"])
                sr["safety_hold"] = bool(o["safety_hold"])
                sr["cooldown"] = bool(o["cooldown"])
    
        def _load_waivers(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("continuity_waivers.csv"))
            self.counts["continuity_waivers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            for r in rows:
                if not (r.get("waiver_signed") or ""):
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                sr = self.site_rows.get((r["event_id"], r["site_id"]))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                sr["cert_pending"] = r["routine_certificate_pending"] == "Y"
                sr["waiver_status"] = r["waiver_status"]
                sr["waiver_signed"] = (r.get("waiver_signed") or "") == "Y"
    
        def _load_rate_cards(self):
            cards = ET.parse(self.p("rate_cards.xml")).getroot().findall("card")
            self.counts["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
            for c in cards:
                sr = self.site_rows.get((c.get("event"), c.get("site")))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                override = c.find("override")
                sr["rate_base"] = int((override if override is not None else 
c.find("base")).text)
                sr["cap"] = int(c.find("cap").text)
    
        def _load_recipient_weights(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.counts["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(rows)
            for r in rows:
                sr = self.site_rows.get((r["event_id"], r["site_id"]))
                if sr is None:
                    continue
                sr["grant_weight"] = int(r["grant_weight"])
    
        def _finish_site_rows(self):
            """Compliance label and effective rate of every site row."""
            for sr in self.site_rows.values():
                waiver_valid = (sr.get("waiver_status") == "VALID" and 
sr.get("waiver_signed")
                                and sr["critical"] and sr["segment"] == 
"public")
                if sr.get("safety_hold"):
                    label = "SAFETY_HOLD"
                elif sr.get("cooldown"):
                    label = "COOLDOWN"
                elif sr.get("cert_pending") and waiver_valid:
                    label = "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
                elif sr.get("cert_pending"):
                    label = "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
                else:
                    label = "CLEAR"
                sr["label"] = label
                raw = (sr["rate_base"] + self.events[sr["event_id"]]["uplift"]
                       + self.segment_adder.get(sr["segment"], 0))
                cap = sr["cap"]
                sr["rate"] = cap if raw > cap else raw
                sr["cap_binding"] = raw > cap
    
        def _load_offers(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("offers.csv"))
            self.counts["offers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.offer_event = {}
            self.offer_rows = []
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                self.offer_event[r["offer_id"]] = r["event_id"]
                if (r["event_id"], r["site_id"]) not in self.site_rows:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.offer_rows.append(r)
    
        def _load_profiles(self):
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            lines = raw_lines(self.p("profiles.tsv"))
            idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(lines[0].split("\t"))}
            i_off, i_itv = idx["offer_id"], idx["interval"]
            i_rel, i_rec, i_mul = idx["relief_wh"], idx["recovery_wh"], 
idx["register_multiplier"]
            self.counts["profiles.tsv"] = len(lines) - 1
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            prof = {}
            for line in lines[1:]:
                f = line.split("\t")
                oid = f[i_off]
                event_id = self.offer_event.get(oid)
                if event_id is None:
                    self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                itv = int(f[i_itv])
                if itv < 1 or itv > self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
                    self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                slot = prof.setdefault(oid, {})
                if itv in slot:
                    self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                mul = int(f[i_mul])
                slot[itv] = (rhu(int(f[i_rel]) * mul, wpt), rhu(int(f[i_rec]) * 
mul, wpt))
            self.profiles = prof
    
        def _load_targets(self):
            rows = csv_rows(self.p("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.counts["targets.tsv"] = len(rows)
            self.targets = {}
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_of_code[r["region_code"].lower()]
                self.targets[(r["event_id"], region, int(r["interval"]))] = (
                    rhu(int(r["target_wh"]), self.wh_per_tick), 
int(r["shortfall_weight"]))
    
        def _load_feeder_limits(self):
            lines = content_lines(self.p("feeder_limits.txt"))
            self.counts["feeder_limits.txt"] = len(lines)
            self.ceilings = {}
            for l in lines:
                region = self.region_of_code[l[10:14].strip().lower()]
                self.ceilings[(l[0:10].strip(), region, int(l[14:17]))] = (
                    rhu(int(l[17:27]), self.wh_per_tick), rhu(int(l[27:37]), 
self.wh_per_tick))
    
        def _load_floors(self):
            with open(self.p("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                floors = json.load(fh)["floors"]
            self.counts["segment_floors.json"] = len(floors)
            self.floors = {}
            for f in floors:
                region = self.region_of_code[f["region_code"].lower()]
                segment = self.segment_of_code[f["segment_code"].lower()]
                self.floors[(f["event_id"], region, segment)] = (
                    rhu(int(f["floor_wh"]), self.wh_per_tick), 
int(f["priority_weight"]))
    
        def _load_pools(self):
            lines = content_lines(self.p("pools.txt"))
            self.counts["pools.txt"] = len(lines)
            self.pools = {}
            for l in lines:
                region = self.region_of_code[l[10:14].strip().lower()]
                self.pools[(l[0:10].strip(), region)] = int(l[14:26])
    
        def _load_allowances(self):
            lines = content_lines(self.p("site_allowances.tsv"))
            self.counts["site_allowances.tsv"] = max(0, len(lines) - 1)
            idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(lines[0].split("\t"))}
            i_site, i_ticks, i_from = idx["site_id"], idx["allowance_ticks"], 
idx["draws_from"]
            self.allowance_ticks = {}
            draws = {}
            for l in lines[1:]:
                f = l.split("\t")
                site_id = f[i_site]
                self.allowance_ticks[site_id] = int(f[i_ticks])
                draws[site_id] = f[i_from].strip() if i_from < len(f) else ""
            root = {}
            for site_id in self.allowance_ticks:
                chain = []
                cur = site_id
                while cur not in root and draws.get(cur):
                    chain.append(cur)
                    cur = draws[cur]
                cur = root.get(cur, cur)
                for s in chain:
                    root[s] = cur
                root[site_id] = cur
            self.allowance_root = root
    
    
    def build_offers(ex):
        """One Offer per non-orphan offer, with interval ticks, totals and 
credit."""
        out = []
        den = 1000 * ex.millicents_per_cent
        wpt = ex.wh_per_tick
        for r in ex.offer_rows:
            sr = ex.site_rows[(r["event_id"], r["site_id"])]
            n = ex.events[r["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
            prof = ex.profiles.get(r["offer_id"], {})
            rate = sr["rate"]
            rel, rec, credit = [], [], 0
            for t in range(1, n + 1):
                a, b = prof.get(t, (0, 0))
                rel.append(a)
                rec.append(b)
                if a > b:
                    credit += rhe((a - b) * wpt * rate, den)
            o = Offer()
            o.oid = r["offer_id"]
            o.event_id = r["event_id"]
            o.site_id = r["site_id"]
            o.bid = int(r["bid_cost_cents"])
            o.segment = sr["segment"]
            o.cap_binding = sr["cap_binding"]
            o.rel = rel
            o.rec = rec
            o.trel = sum(rel)
            o.trec = sum(rec)
            o.credit = credit
            out.append(o)
        return out
    
    
    def solve(ex, event_id, region, site_options):
        """Best feasible portfolio of an event-region; returns (offers, 
tied_count)."""
        ev = ex.events[event_id]
        n = ev["interval_count"]
        target, weight, rel_ceil, rec_ceil = [], [], [], []
        for t in range(1, n + 1):
            tg, w = ex.targets[(event_id, region, t)]
            target.append(tg)
            weight.append(w)
            rc, qc = ex.ceilings[(event_id, region, t)]
            rel_ceil.append(rc)
            rec_ceil.append(qc)
        floors = []
        for seg in ex.segments:
            f, pw = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg), (0, 0))
            if f > 0 and pw > 0:
                floors.append((seg, f, pw))
        seg_index = {seg: i for i, (seg, _f, _w) in enumerate(floors)}
        nseg = len(floors)
        cost_weight = ev["cost_weight"]
        recovery_weight = ev["recovery_weight"]
    
        options = [[None] + sorted(site_options[s], key=lambda o: o.oid)
                   for s in sorted(site_options)]
        depth = len(options)
    
        rel = [0] * n
        rec = [0] * n
        segrel = [0] * nseg
        chosen = []
        acc = [0, 0]  # bid total, recovery tick total
        best = [None, None, 0]
    
        def evaluate():
            shortfall = 0
            over = 0
            for t in range(n):
                d = target[t] - rel[t]
                if d > 0:
                    shortfall += weight[t] * d
                elif d < 0:
                    over -= d
            deficit = 0
            for i in range(nseg):
                d = floors[i][1] - segrel[i]
                if d > 0:
                    deficit += floors[i][2] * d
            key = (shortfall, deficit, cost_weight * acc[0] + recovery_weight * 
acc[1],
                   over, len(chosen))
            if best[0] is None or key < best[0]:
                best[0] = key
                best[1] = tuple(sorted(o.oid for o in chosen))
                best[2] = 1
            elif key == best[0]:
                best[2] += 1
                ids = tuple(sorted(o.oid for o in chosen))
                if ids > best[1]:
                    best[1] = ids
    
        def dfs(i):
            if i == depth:
                evaluate()
                return
            for o in options[i]:
                if o is None:
                    dfs(i + 1)
                    continue
                orel, orec = o.rel, o.rec
                feasible = True
                for t in range(n):
                    if rel[t] + orel[t] > rel_ceil[t] or rec[t] + orec[t] > 
rec_ceil[t]:
                        feasible = False
                        break
                if not feasible:
                    continue
                for t in range(n):
                    rel[t] += orel[t]
                    rec[t] += orec[t]
                si = seg_index.get(o.segment)
                if si is not None:
                    segrel[si] += o.trel
                acc[0] += o.bid
                acc[1] += o.trec
                chosen.append(o)
                dfs(i + 1)
                chosen.pop()
                acc[0] -= o.bid
                acc[1] -= o.trec
                if si is not None:
                    segrel[si] -= o.trel
                for t in range(n):
                    rel[t] -= orel[t]
                    rec[t] -= orec[t]
    
        dfs(0)
        by_id = {o.oid: o for opts in options for o in opts if o is not None}
        return [by_id[i] for i in best[1]], best[2]
    
    
    def largest_remainder(F, weights, order_desc):
        """Apportion F by weight using largest remainder.
    
        order_desc lists the beneficiaries in descending identifier order, which
is how
        equal remainders are served.  Returns (shares, residual cents, residual 
tie).
        """
        W = sum(weights.values())
        shares = {b: F * w // W for b, w in weights.items()}
        rem = {b: (F * w) % W for b, w in weights.items()}
        left = F - sum(shares.values())
        seen = set()
        tie = False
        for r in rem.values():
            if r:
                if r in seen:
                    tie = True
                seen.add(r)
        order = list(order_desc)
        order.sort(key=lambda b: -rem[b])
        for b in order[:left]:
            shares[b] += 1
        return shares, left, tie
    
    
    def compute_grants(ex, pool, carried, credit_cents, site_rows):
        """Fund and apportion one event-region's grant pool."""
        F = pool + carried - credit_cents
        if F < 0:
            F = 0
        weights = {}
        recipients = 0
        for sr in site_rows:
            if "grant_weight" not in sr:
                continue
            recipients += 1
            w = sr["grant_weight"]
            if sr["label"] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                w *= 2
            b = sr["beneficiary_id"]
            weights[b] = weights.get(b, 0) + w
        W = sum(weights.values())
        out = {"pool": pool, "F": F, "allocated": 0, "unallocated": F, "grants":
[],
               "residual": 0, "residual_tie": False,
               "zero_denominator": recipients == 0 or W == 0}
        if out["zero_denominator"]:
            return out
    
        positive = {b: w for b, w in weights.items() if w > 0}
        order_desc = sorted(positive, reverse=True)
        claims = {b: w * ex.claim_cents_per_weight for b, w in positive.items()}
        if sum(claims.values()) <= F:
            shares, residual, tie = claims, 0, False
        else:
            shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(F, positive, order_desc)
            kept = {b: w for b, w in positive.items() if shares[b] >= 
ex.minimum_grant_cents}
            if len(kept) != len(positive):
                if kept:
                    second, residual, tie = largest_remainder(
                        F, kept, [b for b in order_desc if b in kept])
                    shares = {b: second.get(b, 0) for b in positive}
                else:
                    shares, residual, tie = {b: 0 for b in positive}, 0, False
        allocated = sum(shares.values())
        out["allocated"] = allocated
        out["unallocated"] = F - allocated
        out["grants"] = [{"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares[b]} for b in 
sorted(positive)]
        out["residual"] = residual
        out["residual_tie"] = tie
        return out
    
    
    def run(ex):
        """Walk the season in order, choosing portfolios and settling grants."""
        offers = build_offers(ex)
        by_event_region = {}
        for o in offers:
            key = (o.event_id, ex.site_rows[(o.event_id, o.site_id)]["region"])
            by_event_region.setdefault(key, []).append(o)
        rows_by_event_region = {}
        for sr in ex.site_rows.values():
            rows_by_event_region.setdefault((sr["event_id"], sr["region"]), 
[]).append(sr)
    
        remaining = {r: ex.allowance_ticks[r] for r in 
set(ex.allowance_root.values())}
        carried = {r: 0 for r in ex.regions}
        stats = {"ineligible": 0, "blocked": 0, "empty_groups": 0, 
"cap_binding": 0,
                 "residual": 0, "residual_tie_pools": 0, "unallocated_pools": 0}
        results = {}
    
        for event_id in ex.season_order:
            n = ex.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
            consumed = []
            for region in ex.regions:
                key = (event_id, region)
                site_options = {}
                for o in by_event_region.get(key, []):
                    sr = ex.site_rows[(o.event_id, o.site_id)]
                    if not sr.get("dispatch_authorized"):
                        stats["ineligible"] += 1
                        continue
                    if o.trel > remaining.get(ex.allowance_root.get(o.site_id), 
0):
                        stats["blocked"] += 1
                        continue
                    site_options.setdefault(o.site_id, []).append(o)
                candidate_count = sum(len(v) for v in site_options.values())
                if candidate_count == 0:
                    stats["empty_groups"] += 1
                chosen, tied = solve(ex, event_id, region, site_options)
                consumed.extend(chosen)
    
                rel = [0] * n
                rec = [0] * n
                bid = credit = 0
                for o in chosen:
                    for t in range(n):
                        rel[t] += o.rel[t]
                        rec[t] += o.rec[t]
                    bid += o.bid
                    credit += o.credit
                    if o.cap_binding:
                        stats["cap_binding"] += 1
    
                site_rows = rows_by_event_region.get(key, ())
                g = compute_grants(ex, ex.pools.get(key, 0), carried[region], 
credit, site_rows)
                carried[region] = g["unallocated"]
                stats["residual"] += g["residual"]
                stats["residual_tie_pools"] += 1 if g["residual_tie"] else 0
                stats["unallocated_pools"] += 1 if g["zero_denominator"] else 0
    
                counts = {lab: 0 for lab in LABELS}
                for sr in site_rows:
                    counts[sr["label"]] += 1
    
                results[key] = {
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
                    "chosen": chosen,
                    "rel": rel,
                    "rec": rec,
                    "bid": bid,
                    "credit": credit,
                    "grants": g,
                    "compliance_counts": counts,
                    "tied": tied,
                }
            for o in consumed:
                root = ex.allowance_root.get(o.site_id)
                if root in remaining:
                    remaining[root] -= o.trel
        return results, stats
    
    
    REGION_TOTAL_KEYS = ("event_count", "candidate_offer_count", 
"selected_offer_count",
                         "relief_ticks", "recovery_ticks", "shortfall_ticks",
                         "overdelivery_ticks", "bid_cost_cents", "credit_cents",
                         "grant_pool_cents", "grant_funded_cents", 
"grant_allocated_cents",
                         "grant_unallocated_cents")
    
    
    def region_doc(ex, region, results):
        events_out = []
        totals = {k: 0 for k in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS}
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            r = results[(event_id, region)]
            intervals = []
            rel_sum = rec_sum = short_sum = over_sum = 0
            for t in range(1, ex.events[event_id]["interval_count"] + 1):
                tg = ex.targets[(event_id, region, t)][0]
                rl = r["rel"][t - 1]
                rc = r["rec"][t - 1]
                short = tg - rl if tg > rl else 0
                over = rl - tg if rl > tg else 0
                intervals.append({"interval": t, "target_ticks": tg, 
"relief_ticks": rl,
                                  "recovery_ticks": rc, "shortfall_ticks": 
short,
                                  "overdelivery_ticks": over})
                rel_sum += rl
                rec_sum += rc
                short_sum += short
                over_sum += over
            g = r["grants"]
            events_out.append({
                "event_id": event_id,
                "candidate_offer_count": r["candidate_offer_count"],
                "selected_offer_ids": sorted(o.oid for o in r["chosen"]),
                "intervals": intervals,
                "bid_cost_cents": r["bid"],
                "credit_cents": r["credit"],
                "grant_pool_cents": g["pool"],
                "grant_funded_cents": g["F"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": g["allocated"],
                "grant_unallocated_cents": g["unallocated"],
                "grants": g["grants"],
                "compliance_counts": r["compliance_counts"],
            })
            totals["event_count"] += 1
            totals["candidate_offer_count"] += r["candidate_offer_count"]
            totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(r["chosen"])
            totals["relief_ticks"] += rel_sum
            totals["recovery_ticks"] += rec_sum
            totals["shortfall_ticks"] += short_sum
            totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += over_sum
            totals["bid_cost_cents"] += r["bid"]
            totals["credit_cents"] += r["credit"]
            totals["grant_pool_cents"] += g["pool"]
            totals["grant_funded_cents"] += g["F"]
            totals["grant_allocated_cents"] += g["allocated"]
            totals["grant_unallocated_cents"] += g["unallocated"]
        return {"region": region, "events": events_out, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def segment_doc(ex, segment, results):
        entries = []
        totals = {k: 0 for k in ("selected_offer_count", "relief_ticks", 
"recovery_ticks",
                                 "bid_cost_cents", "credit_cents", 
"floor_ticks")}
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                chosen = [o for o in results[(event_id, region)]["chosen"]
                          if o.segment == segment]
                rel = sum(o.trel for o in chosen)
                rec = sum(o.trec for o in chosen)
                bid = sum(o.bid for o in chosen)
                credit = sum(o.credit for o in chosen)
                floor = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0]
                bp = 10000 if floor == 0 else min(10000, rhu(10000 * rel, 
floor))
                entries.append({
                    "event_id": event_id, "region": region,
                    "selected_offer_ids": sorted(o.oid for o in chosen),
                    "relief_ticks": rel, "recovery_ticks": rec,
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid, "credit_cents": credit,
                    "floor_ticks": floor, "floor_attainment_bp": bp,
                })
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(chosen)
                totals["relief_ticks"] += rel
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += rec
                totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid
                totals["credit_cents"] += credit
                totals["floor_ticks"] += floor
        return {"segment": segment, "event_regions": entries, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def summary_doc(ex, region_docs, segment_docs):
        keys = [k for k in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS if k != "event_count"]
        doc = {k: 0 for k in keys}
        for region in ex.regions:
            t = region_docs[region]["totals"]
            for k in keys:
                doc[k] += t[k]
        doc["event_count"] = len(ex.event_ids)
        doc["event_region_count"] = len(ex.event_ids) * len(ex.regions)
        doc["site_count"] = len(ex.site_rows)
        doc["regions"] = {
            region: {k: region_docs[region]["totals"][k] for k in
                     ("selected_offer_count", "relief_ticks", "bid_cost_cents",
                      "credit_cents", "grant_allocated_cents")}
            for region in ex.regions}
        doc["segments"] = {
            segment: {k: segment_docs[segment]["totals"][k] for k in
                      ("selected_offer_count", "relief_ticks", "bid_cost_cents",
                       "credit_cents")}
            for segment in ex.segments}
        return doc
    
    
    def policy_doc(ex, results, stats):
        counts = {lab: 0 for lab in LABELS}
        labels = []
        for key in sorted(ex.site_rows):
            sr = ex.site_rows[key]
            counts[sr["label"]] += 1
            labels.append({"event_id": sr["event_id"], "site_id": sr["site_id"],
                           "label": sr["label"]})
        ties = []
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                r = results[(event_id, region)]
                if r["tied"] >= 2:
                    ties.append({"event_id": event_id, "region": region,
                                 "tied_portfolio_count": r["tied"],
                                 "selected_offer_ids": sorted(o.oid for o in 
r["chosen"])})
        return {
            "compliance_counts": counts,
            "site_labels": labels,
            "tie_decisions": ties,
            "cap_binding_count": stats["cap_binding"],
            "residual_cents": stats["residual"],
            "residual_tie_pool_count": stats["residual_tie_pools"],
        }
    
    
    def quality_doc(ex, stats):
        zero_floor = 0
        for event_id in ex.event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                for segment in ex.segments:
                    if ex.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))[0] == 
0:
                        zero_floor += 1
        return {
            "rows": dict(ex.counts),
            "orphan_offer_rows": ex.orphan_offer_rows,
            "orphan_profile_rows": ex.orphan_profile_rows,
            "out_of_range_profile_rows": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,
            "duplicate_profile_rows": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,
            "unknown_region_site_rows": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,
            "waiver_signed_missing": ex.waiver_signed_missing,
            "ineligible_offer_count": stats["ineligible"],
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": stats["blocked"],
            "empty_candidate_groups": stats["empty_groups"],
            "zero_floor_count": zero_floor,
            "unallocated_pool_count": stats["unallocated_pools"],
        }
    
    
    def write_json(path, doc):
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(doc, fh, sort_keys=True, indent=1)
            fh.write("\n")
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            print("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        data_dir, out_dir = argv[1], argv[2]
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        ex = Extract(data_dir)
        results, stats = run(ex)
        region_docs = {r: region_doc(ex, r, results) for r in ex.regions}
        segment_docs = {s: segment_doc(ex, s, results) for s in ex.segments}
        for region, doc in region_docs.items():
            write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "region_%s.json" % region), doc)
        for segment, doc in segment_docs.items():
            write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "segment_%s.json" % segment), doc)
        write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"),
                   summary_doc(ex, region_docs, segment_docs))
        write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "policy_audit.json"), policy_doc(ex, 
results, stats))
        write_json(os.path.join(out_dir, "data_quality.json"), quality_doc(ex, 
stats))
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

+187|    def _finish_site_rows(self):
+188|        """Compliance label and effective rate of every site row."""
+189|        for sr in self.site_rows.values():
+190|            waiver_valid = (sr.get("waiver_status") == "VALID" and 
sr.get("waiver_signed")
+191|                            and sr["critical"] and sr["segment"] == 
"public")
+192|            if sr.get("safety_hold"):
+193|                label = "SAFETY_HOLD"
+194|            elif sr.get("cooldown"):
+195|                label = "COOLDOWN"
+196|            elif sr.get("cert_pending") and waiver_valid:
+197|                label = "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
+198|            elif sr.get("cert_pending"):
+199|                label = "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
+200|            else:
+201|                label = "CLEAR"
+202|            sr["label"] = label
+203|            raw = (sr["rate_base"] + self.events[sr["event_id"]]["uplift"]
+204|                   + self.segment_adder.get(sr["segment"], 0))
+205|            cap = sr["cap"]
+206|            sr["rate"] = cap if raw > cap else raw
+207|            sr["cap_binding"] = raw > cap

+209|    def _load_offers(self):
+210|        rows = csv_rows(self.p("offers.csv"))
+211|        self.counts["offers.csv"] = len(rows)
+212|        self.offer_event = {}
+213|        self.offer_rows = []
+214|        self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
+215|        for r in rows:
+216|            self.offer_event[r["offer_id"]] = r["event_id"]
+217|            if (r["event_id"], r["site_id"]) not in self.site_rows:
+218|                self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
+219|                continue

+222|    def _load_profiles(self):
+223|        wpt = self.wh_per_tick
+224|        lines = raw_lines(self.p("profiles.tsv"))
+225|        idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(lines[0].split("\t"))}
+226|        i_off, i_itv = idx["offer_id"], idx["interval"]
+227|        i_rel, i_rec, i_mul = idx["relief_wh"], idx["recovery_wh"], 
idx["register_multiplier"]
+228|        self.counts["profiles.tsv"] = len(lines) - 1
+229|        self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
+230|        self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
+231|        self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
+232|        prof = {}
+233|        for line in lines[1:]:
+234|            f = line.split("\t")
+235|            oid = f[i_off]
+236|            event_id = self.offer_event.get(oid)
+237|            if event_id is None:
+238|                self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
+239|                continue
+240|            itv = int(f[i_itv])
+241|            if itv < 1 or itv > self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
+242|                self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
+243|                continue
+244|            slot = prof.setdefault(oid, {})
+245|            if itv in slot:
+246|                self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
+247|                continue
+248|            mul = int(f[i_mul])
+249|            slot[itv] = (rhu(int(f[i_rel]) * mul, wpt), rhu(int(f[i_rec]) *
mul, wpt))
+250|        self.profiles = prof
+251|

+258|            self.targets[(r["event_id"], region, int(r["interval"]))] = (
+259|                rhu(int(r["target_wh"]), self.wh_per_tick), 
int(r["shortfall_weight"]))
